([Shash-Builds/Shash.builds](https://github.com/Shash-Builds/Shash.builds/tree/HEAD/tools/toolbox/transcribe) · ★1)
- **transcribe**: Transcribe audio and video files using Audetic whisper service. Use when: (1) User wants to transcribe an audio or video file (2) User has a recording and needs a text transcript (3) User wants transcript analysis (gaps, speech rate, silence detection) (4) User wants to transcribe all media files in a directory Triggers: "transcribe", "transcription", "transcript", "speech to text", "audio to text", "transcribe audio", "transcribe video", "transcribe recording" ([silvabyte/skills](https://github.com/silvabyte/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/transcribe) · ★1)
- **transcribe**: Create clean, subtitle-ready `.srt` subtitles for a local audio or video file using Whisper CLI. ([thesammykins/skills](https://github.com/thesammykins/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/transcribe) · ★1)
- **transcribe**: Transcribe speech from authorized audio or video recordings, optionally with timestamps or speaker labels, and verify uncertain passages against the recording. ([Tom-R-Main/siftable-skills](https://github.com/Tom-R-Main/siftable-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/transcribe) · ★1)
- **transcribe**: Transcribe audio or video with an already configured local or approved speech-to-text capability, selecting current supported options from evidence at run. - **transcribe**: 视频/音频转文字。用 Whisper 将视频或音频文件转写为带时间戳的文字。支持模型选择、音频拆分、自动代理。Apple Silicon 上使用 GPU 加速。 ([1whistlerrrr/transcribe-plugin](https://github.com/1whistlerrrr/transcribe-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/transcribe))
- **transcribe**: Turn a meeting Recording into a Transcript that says who said what, do the Naming with the user, show Speaker stats, on request write a Clean Transcript, and write the Summary and Minutes. - **transcribe**: Transcribe audio from file or microphone. Russian + IT slang optimized. Auto-chunks long files. Local MLX whisper (default, offline, free, Russian fine-tune), OpenAI gpt-4o-mini-transcribe, or local/cloud speaker diarization. Use when user asks to transcribe, convert speech to text, or record and transcribe audio. ALSO auto-activate (no "transcribe" word needed) whenever the user sends, pastes, or links a path/URL to an audio file — .m4a (most common), .mp3, .wav, .caf, .ogg, .flac — or a video file (.mp4, .mov, .webm, .mkv); a bare audio path/link means "transcribe this". - **transcribe**: Transcribe audio from file or microphone. Russian + IT slang optimized. Auto-chunks long files.
